        start_snippet = beacon.get("start_snippet")
        comment_line = beacon.get("comment_line") or 0

        attachment_line: Optional[int] = None

        if start_snippet:
            norm_snip = normalize_for_match(start_snippet)
            if not norm_snip:
                continue
            start_candidates: list[int] = []
            for idx, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
                stripped = line.lstrip()
                if stripped.startswith("//") or stripped.startswith("/*") or stripped.startswith("*") or stripped.startswith("*/"):
                    continue
                if norm_snip in normalize_for_match(line):
                    start_candidates.append(idx)
            if not start_candidates:
                continue
            if len(start_candidates) == 1:
                attachment_line = start_candidates[0]
            else:
                after = [ln for ln in start_candidates if ln > comment_line]
                if len(after) == 1:
                    attachment_line = after[0]
                else:
                    continue
        else:
            # Snippet-less spans attach based on beacon location so root-level
            # documentation spans stay at file scope instead of slipping under
            # the next AST node.
            span_start = beacon.get("span_lineno_start")
            if isinstance(span_start, int):
                attachment_line = span_start
            elif isinstance(comment_line, int) and comment_line > 0:
                attachment_line = comment_line
            else:
                anchor = beacon.get("_anchor_lineno")
                attachment_line = anchor if isinstance(anchor, int) else None

        if attachment_line is None:
            continue

        enclosing = _find_enclosing_ast_node_for_line(outline_nodes, attachment_line)

        b_id = (beacon.get("id") or "").strip()
        role = (beacon.get("role") or "").strip()
        raw_slice_labels = (beacon.get("slice_labels") or "").strip()
        if not role and b_id and "@" in b_id:
            role = b_id.split("@", 1)[0]
        slice_labels = _canonicalize_slice_labels(raw_slice_labels, role)

        show_span, show_span_explicit = _beacon_show_span(beacon)

        display_role = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(role) or b_id or "js-span-beacon"
        display_slice = slice_labels or "-"
        tag_suffix = _slice_label_tags(slice_labels or display_slice)

        name = f"🔱 {display_role}"
        if tag_suffix:
            name = f"{name} {tag_suffix}"

        context_lines = _extract_js_beacon_context(comment_line)
        comment = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(beacon.get("comment"))

        note_lines = [
            "BEACON (JS/TS SPAN)",
            f"id: {b_id}",
            f"role: {role}",
            f"slice_labels: {slice_labels}",
            "kind: span",
        ]
        if show_span_explicit:
            note_lines.append(f"show_span: {'true' if show_span else 'false'}")
        if comment:
            note_lines.append(f"comment: {comment}")
        if context_lines:
            note_lines.append("")
            note_lines.append("CONTEXT COMMENTS (JS/TS):")
            note_lines.extend(context_lines)

        # Raw JS/TS span text between @beacon[...] and @beacon-close[...] (if available)
        span_start = beacon.get("span_lineno_start")
        span_end = beacon.get("span_lineno_end")
        span_text_lines: List[str] = []
        if isinstance(span_start, int) and isinstance(span_end, int):
            span_start = max(1, span_start)
            span_end = min(n, span_end)
            if span_start <= span_end:
                span_text_lines = lines[span_start - 1 : span_end]
                # Trim leading/trailing blank lines inside the span
                while span_text_lines and not span_text_lines[0].strip():
                    span_text_lines.pop(0)
                while span_text_lines and not span_text_lines[-1].strip():
                    span_text_lines.pop()

        if show_span and span_text_lines:
            note_lines.append("")
            note_lines.append("SPAN TEXT:")
            note_lines.extend(span_text_lines)

        span_node = {
            "name": name,
            "note": "\n".join(note_lines),
            "children": [],
            "orig_lineno_start_unused": attachment_line,
            "orig_lineno_end_unused": attachment_line,
        }

        if enclosing is not None:
            children = enclosing.get("children") or []
            _insert_child_in_source_order(children, span_node, attachment_line)
            enclosing["children"] = children
        else:
            _insert_child_in_source_order(outline_nodes, span_node, attachment_line)


# @beacon[
#   id=carto-js-ts@split_name_and_tags,
#   role=🔱 carto-js-ts,
#   slice_labels=carto-js-ts,carto-js-ts-beacons,ra-reconcile,nexus--tags,
#   kind=span,
# ]
# Utility: split a Workflowy node name into base text and trailing #tags.
# Used by update_beacon_from_node_* helpers to derive slice_labels from
# name suffixes while keeping the canonical AST/beacon node name clean.
# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@split_name_and_tags-t17m,
#   role=split_name_and_tags,
#   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def _token_is_pure_decoration(token: str) -> bool:
    """True iff ``token`` is a pure trailing-region DECORATION glyph run.

    Decoration = the Cartographer trident (\U0001F531 🔱), the marker/anchor
    emoji family (\U0001FA9A 🪚 and friends), and any token that contains NO
    alphanumeric character at all (i.e. it is entirely emoji / symbol /
    punctuation). This is what lets the trailing-tag walker treat a
    ``base 🔱 #tag`` region as ``base`` + ``[#tag]`` (trident absorbed as a
    delimiter, NOT left glued onto the base) -- the fix for the piled-up
    trident bug where two parsers disagreed on where the base ends and the
    reconcile writer kept re-adding a trident onto a base that still carried
    one.

    A token that mixes alphanumerics with emoji (e.g. real content words, or a
    heading that legitimately ends in a word) is NOT decoration and is left in
    the base.
    """
    if not token:
        return False
    return not any(ch.isalnum() for ch in token)


# @beacon[
#   id=agent-kit@split_name_and_tags,
#   slice_labels=agent-kit-vendor,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=The one name splitter: peels the trailing tag region (and any leading decoration) off a mapped node name, shared by NEXUS's selection and the repository view's titles; an agent-kit build root,
# ]

def split_name_and_tags(raw_name: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    name = (raw_name or "").strip()
    if not name:
        return "", []
    tokens = name.split()
    tags: list[str] = []
    # Walk from the end, collect tokens that look like tags (start with '#').
    # Workflowy may expose rendered tags through /nodes-export with visual
    # underline markup embedded in the NAME (e.g. '#<u>tag</u>' or
    # '#tag<u>-🏷️</u>'). That markup is not semantic tag content, so strip it
    # before deciding whether the token is a trailing #tag and before passing
    # it to slice-label canonicalization.
    #
    # EMOJI/TRIDENT-AWARE TRAILING REGION (Dan, 2026 -- the piled-up-trident
    # fix). The trailing tag REGION is a contiguous run of (a) '#'-tags and
    # (b) pure-decoration glyph tokens (the trident 🔱, marker emoji, or any
    # non-alphanumeric-only token) that sits AMONG / BESIDE those tags. We peel
    # that whole run, but:
    #   * ``tags`` returns ONLY the '#'-tags -- NEVER any emoji/trident. The
    #     trident is presentation the normalizer re-synthesizes on write; it
    #     must never survive as tag content.
    #   * a trailing decoration token is peeled ONLY when the region also
    #     contains at least one real '#'-tag OR a trident/marker glyph. A bare
    #     trailing CONTENT emoji with no tags in the run (e.g. 'Ship it 🚀',
    #     'Done ✅') is LEFT in the base -- we must not silently eat a
    #     deliberate emoji suffix on a heading. (Adjacency rule: decoration is
    #     only decoration when it's decorating a tag/trident region.)
    # A leading run of decoration/trident on the base is NOT touched here (that
    # is a separate concern handled by the name normalizer); this function owns
    # only the TRAILING region.
    #
    # ALGORITHM (single right-to-left scan; simple + obviously correct):
    # Walk tokens from the end. Track ``region_start`` = the index of the
    # last CONFIRMED trailing-region token (a '#'-tag, the trident, or a
    # marker emoji). Pure-decoration tokens that are NOT markers (generic
    # non-alnum tokens) are provisionally skipped but only COMMITTED to the
    # region if a confirmed tag/marker is found deeper toward the base. A
    # real content token ends the scan. At the end, the region is
    # ``tokens[region_start:]``; if nothing was confirmed, there is no
    # trailing region and the whole name is the base (content-emoji safety:
    # 'Ship it 🚀' has no tag/marker, so 🚀 stays in the base).
    # Use the module-global TRIDENT constant (defined above) -- never a local
    # literal -- so the glyph has exactly one home in this file.
    trident = TRIDENT

    def _is_marker_decoration(tok: str) -> bool:
        t = tok.strip()
        return t == trident or t.startswith("\U0001FA9A")

    region_start = len(tokens)  # exclusive-of-region default (no region yet)
    i = len(tokens) - 1
    while i >= 0:
        candidate = _strip_workflowy_tag_underline_markup(tokens[i])
        if candidate.startswith("#") or _is_marker_decoration(candidate):
            # Confirmed region member: the region extends at least to here,
            # AND everything between here and the previously-confirmed start
            # (any provisional generic decoration we skipped) is now committed.
            region_start = i
            i -= 1
            continue
        if _token_is_pure_decoration(candidate):
            # Provisional: part of the region ONLY if a confirmed tag/marker
            # lies deeper. Keep scanning without moving region_start; a later
            # confirmed member will pull region_start past this token.
            i -= 1
            continue
        # Real content token -> the trailing region ends here.
        break

    if region_start >= len(tokens):
        # No confirmed tag/marker anywhere in the trailing run -> no region.
        return name, []
    base = " ".join(tokens[:region_start]).strip()
    for tok in tokens[region_start:]:
        candidate = _strip_workflowy_tag_underline_markup(tok)
        if candidate.startswith("#"):
            tags.append(candidate)
        # decoration tokens (trident / markers / generic emoji) are DISCARDED
        # -- never returned as tags (presentation, re-synthesized on write).
    return base, tags


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file-9q2m,
#   role=ƒ normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file(file_path: str),
#   slice_labels=ra-reconcile,carto-js-ts-beacons,carto-fwd-source,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Canonicalize legacy beacon metadata alias comments= to comment= during F12 flows.,
# ]
def normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file(file_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Normalize legacy beacon key alias `comments=` -> `comment=` inside real beacon metadata blocks only.

    This hygiene pass is used by F12 refresh/update flows so users can type
    either field name and the source is silently canonicalized.

    SAFETY PRINCIPLE
    ----------------
    We must never perform a whole-file `comments=` replacement, because real
    source code can legitimately contain assignments such as:

        comments = EXCLUDED.comments

    Therefore we only rewrite lines that are inside a syntactically recognized
    ``@beacon[...]`` metadata block for the file's language/comment style.
    """
    try:
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            original = f.read()
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        return {"success": False, "changed": False, "file_path": file_path, "error": str(e)}

    ext = os.path.splitext(file_path)[1].lower()
    lines = original.splitlines(keepends=True)
    if not lines:
        return {"success": True, "changed": False, "file_path": file_path}

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_prefixed_body-7i23,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_prefixed_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_prefixed_body(raw: str, markers: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
        stripped = raw.lstrip()
        indent_len = len(raw) - len(stripped)
        for marker in markers:
            if stripped.startswith(marker):
                remainder = stripped[len(marker):]
                ws_len = len(remainder) - len(remainder.lstrip())
                prefix_len = indent_len + len(marker) + ws_len
                return raw[:prefix_len], raw[prefix_len:]
        return None

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_hash_body-tujh,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_hash_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_hash_body(raw: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
        return _extract_prefixed_body(raw, ("#",))

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_dashdash_body-wke2,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_dashdash_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_dashdash_body(raw: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
        return _extract_prefixed_body(raw, ("--",))

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_slashslash_body-l4zr,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_slashslash_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_slashslash_body(raw: str) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
        return _extract_prefixed_body(raw, ("//",))

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_cblock_body-uuaa,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_cblock_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_cblock_body(raw: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        stripped = raw.lstrip()
        indent_len = len(raw) - len(stripped)
        if stripped.startswith("/*"):
            remainder = stripped[2:]
            ws_len = len(remainder) - len(remainder.lstrip())
            prefix_len = indent_len + 2 + ws_len
            return raw[:prefix_len], raw[prefix_len:]
        if stripped.startswith("*/"):
            remainder = stripped[2:]
            ws_len = len(remainder) - len(remainder.lstrip())
            prefix_len = indent_len + 2 + ws_len
            return raw[:prefix_len], raw[prefix_len:]
        if stripped.startswith("*"):
            remainder = stripped[1:]
            ws_len = len(remainder) - len(remainder.lstrip())
            prefix_len = indent_len + 1 + ws_len
            return raw[:prefix_len], raw[prefix_len:]
        # For multiline /* ... */ beacon blocks, inner lines may be bare text.
        return raw[:indent_len], raw[indent_len:]

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_plain_body-mi3n,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._extract_plain_body,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _extract_plain_body(raw: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        stripped = raw.lstrip()
        indent_len = len(raw) - len(stripped)
        return raw[:indent_len], raw[indent_len:]

    # @beacon[
    #   id=auto-beacon@normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._replace_comments_key-30xl,
    #   role=normalize_beacon_comment_field_aliases_in_file._replace_comments_key,
    #   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
    #   kind=ast,
    # ]
    def _replace_comments_key(raw: str, extractor) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        parts = extractor(raw)
        if parts is None:
            return raw, False
        prefix, body = parts
        new_body, n = re.subn(r"^comments(\s*=)", r"comment\1", body, count=1)
        if n == 0:
            return raw, False
        return prefix + new_body, True

    py_like_exts = {".py", ".sh", ".bash", ".yml", ".yaml"}
    js_like_exts = {".js", ".jsx", ".ts", ".tsx", ".vue"}
    html_comment_exts = {".md", ".markdown", ".vue"}

    mode: str | None = None
    pending_html_comment = False
    changed = False

    for idx, raw in enumerate(lines):
        if mode is None:
            if ext in py_like_exts:
                parts = _extract_hash_body(raw)
                if parts and parts[1].startswith("@beacon["):
                    mode = "hash"
                continue

            if ext == ".sql":
                parts = _extract_dashdash_body(raw)
                if parts and parts[1].startswith("@beacon["):
                    mode = "dashdash"
                continue

            if ext in js_like_exts:
                parts = _extract_slashslash_body(raw)
                if parts and parts[1].startswith("@beacon["):
                    mode = "slashslash"
                    continue
                cblock_parts = _extract_cblock_body(raw)
                if cblock_parts[1].startswith("@beacon["):
                    mode = "cblock"
                    continue

            if ext in html_comment_exts:
                stripped = raw.strip()
                if pending_html_comment:
                    pending_html_comment = False
                    if stripped.startswith("@beacon["):
                        mode = "html_comment"
                    continue
                if stripped.startswith("<!--"):
                    after = stripped[len("<!--"):].lstrip()
                    if after.startswith("@beacon["):
                        mode = "html_comment"
                    else:
                        pending_html_comment = True
                continue

            continue

        if mode == "hash":
            new_raw, did_change = _replace_comments_key(raw, _extract_hash_body)
            lines[idx] = new_raw
            changed = changed or did_change
            parts = _extract_hash_body(new_raw)
            if parts and parts[1].strip().startswith("]"):
                mode = None
            continue

        if mode == "dashdash":
            new_raw, did_change = _replace_comments_key(raw, _extract_dashdash_body)
            lines[idx] = new_raw
            changed = changed or did_change
            parts = _extract_dashdash_body(new_raw)
            if parts and parts[1].strip().startswith("]"):
                mode = None
            continue

        if mode == "slashslash":
            new_raw, did_change = _replace_comments_key(raw, _extract_slashslash_body)
            lines[idx] = new_raw
            changed = changed or did_change
            parts = _extract_slashslash_body(new_raw)
            if parts and parts[1].strip().startswith("]"):
                mode = None
            continue

        if mode == "cblock":
            new_raw, did_change = _replace_comments_key(raw, _extract_cblock_body)
            lines[idx] = new_raw
            changed = changed or did_change
            _prefix, body = _extract_cblock_body(new_raw)
            if body.strip().startswith("]"):
                mode = None
            continue

        if mode == "html_comment":
            new_raw, did_change = _replace_comments_key(raw, _extract_plain_body)
            lines[idx] = new_raw
            changed = changed or did_change
            _prefix, body = _extract_plain_body(new_raw)
            if body.strip().startswith("]"):
                mode = None
            continue

    if not changed:
        return {"success": True, "changed": False, "file_path": file_path}

    normalized = "".join(lines)
    try:
        with open(file_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(normalized)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        return {"success": False, "changed": False, "file_path": file_path, "error": str(e)}

    return {"success": True, "changed": True, "file_path": file_path}


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_indent_beacon_block-6lcl,
#   role=_indent_beacon_block,
#   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,agent-kit-vendor,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def _indent_beacon_block(block_lines: list[str], source_lines: list[str], insert_idx: int) -> list[str]:
    """Indent beacon block to match the following non-blank line's indentation.
    
    Args:
        block_lines: The beacon comment block lines (unindented).
        source_lines: The full source file lines array.
        insert_idx: The line index where the beacon will be inserted.
    
    Returns:
        The beacon block with indentation applied.
    """
    # Scan forward from insert_idx to find first non-blank line.
    indent = ""
    for idx in range(insert_idx, len(source_lines)):
        line = source_lines[idx]
        if line.strip():
            # Measure leading whitespace.
            indent = line[:len(line) - len(line.lstrip())]
            break
    # Apply indent to every line in the block.
    return [indent + line for line in block_lines]


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_generate_auto_beacon_hash-22qn,
#   role=_generate_auto_beacon_hash,
#   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def _generate_auto_beacon_hash() -> str:
    """Generate a short random hash for auto-beacon collision resistance.
    
    Returns a 4-character lowercase alphanumeric string.
    """
    return "".join(random.choices(string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits, k=4))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lightning Rod beacon mutation -- PLAN SEAM disk helpers (SPEC v5 §5)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#
# PURE readers only: these helpers NEVER write to disk (invariant 5 --
# zero preliminary writes) and NEVER call a parser entry point that may
# rewrite its input (§7: parse_markdown_structure is forbidden on this path).


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_parse_disk_beacons_for_language-pdsk,
#   role=_parse_disk_beacons_for_language(language, source_path),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=PURE disk read+beacon-parse for the Lightning Rod mutation plan seam (SPEC v5 §5 step 4). Returns (lines, beacons) for any of the 7 beacon languages. The Markdown control-char sanitize is IN-MEMORY ONLY here (zero preliminary writes, §7) and parse_markdown_structure (which may rewrite its input) is NEVER called from this path. Vue files are parsed with BOTH the JS parser (script // + style /* */ forms) and the Markdown parser (template <!-- --> form) so id-cardinality checks see every beacon in the file.,
# ]
def _parse_disk_beacons_for_language(language: str, source_path: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[dict]]:
    """Read + parse a file's beacon blocks PURELY (no writes, no mutating parsers).

    Returns ``(lines, beacons)``. The Markdown control-char sanitize is
    IN-MEMORY ONLY on this path (SPEC v5 §7 zero-preliminary-writes), and
    ``parse_markdown_structure`` (which may rewrite its input) is NEVER
    called here.
    """
    if language == "markdown":
        with open(source_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            raw_text = f.read()
        safe_text = _strip_disallowed_markdown_control_chars(raw_text)
        lines = _markdown_source_lines(safe_text)
        return lines, parse_markdown_beacon_blocks(lines)
    with open(source_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lines = f.read().splitlines()
    if language == "python":
        return lines, parse_python_beacon_blocks(lines)
    if language == "js_ts":
        return lines, parse_js_beacon_blocks(lines)
    if language == "vue":
        # Script beacons (// style) + style blocks (/* */ style) via the JS
        # parser; template beacons (<!-- --> style) via the Markdown parser,
        # so id-cardinality checks see every beacon in the file.
        return lines, parse_js_beacon_blocks(lines) + parse_markdown_beacon_blocks(lines)
    if language == "sql":
        return lines, parse_sql_beacon_blocks(lines)
    if language == "shell":
        return lines, parse_sh_beacon_blocks(lines)
    if language == "yaml":
        return lines, parse_yaml_beacon_blocks(lines)
    raise ValueError(f"unknown beacon language {language!r}")


_BEACON_EXTENT_LINE_PREFIX = {
    "python": "#",
    "js_ts": "//",
    "vue": "//",
    "sql": "--",
    "shell": "#",
    "yaml": "#",
}


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_beacon_block_extent_for_language-ext1,
#   role=_beacon_block_extent_for_language(language, lines, beacon),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,agent-kit-vendor,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Compute the 0-based inclusive (start_idx, end_idx) extent of one parsed beacon block. Markdown/HTML: comment_line is the @beacon[ line; the block runs from the nearest preceding <!-- (blank-line tolerant) through the matching -->. Line-comment languages (python/js_ts/vue-script/sql/shell/yaml): the block runs from the @beacon[ line through the line whose prefix-stripped body is exactly ]. Shared by the plan seam's anchor reconciliation and the commit seam's verification.,
# ]
def _beacon_block_extent_for_language(
    language: str,
    lines: list[str],
    beacon: dict,
) -> Optional[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Return the 0-based inclusive ``(start_idx, end_idx)`` extent of one
    parsed beacon block, or ``None`` when it cannot be computed."""
    cl = beacon.get("comment_line")
    if not isinstance(cl, int) or cl <= 0 or cl > len(lines):
        return None
    if language == "markdown":
        start_idx = cl - 1
        j = cl - 1
        while j >= 1:
            raw = lines[j - 1]
            if "<!--" in raw:
                start_idx = j - 1
                break
            if raw.strip():
                break
            j -= 1
        end_idx = None
        j = cl
        while j <= len(lines):
            raw = lines[j - 1]
            if "-->" in raw:
                end_idx = j - 1
                break
            j += 1
        if end_idx is None:
            # Unterminated block (malformed source): fail CLOSED rather than
            # fabricate an extent reaching unintended content.
            return None
        return start_idx, end_idx
    prefix = _BEACON_EXTENT_LINE_PREFIX.get(language)
    if prefix is None:
        return None
    start_idx = cl - 1
    end_idx = None
    j = cl
    while j <= len(lines):
        raw = lines[j - 1]
        stripped = raw.lstrip()
        body = stripped[len(prefix):].lstrip() if stripped.startswith(prefix) else stripped
        if body == "]":
            end_idx = j - 1
            break
        j += 1
    if end_idx is None:
        # Unterminated block (malformed source): fail CLOSED rather than
        # fabricate an extent reaching unintended content.
        return None
    return start_idx, end_idx


# @beacon[
#   id=carto-python@decorator-ranges-by-definition,
#   slice_labels=carto-fwd-source,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=ONE pure Python decorator-syntax authority shared by forward beacon attachment, outline/comment-context starts, and reverse beacon identity matching. AST ownership plus tokenizer logical-line boundaries covers stacked/multiline/parenthesized decorators. Builds a whole-file index once; callers may supply the AST from the same source snapshot to avoid reparsing. No I/O, evaluation, mutation, or attachment policy.,
# ]
def _python_decorator_ranges_by_definition(
    lines: list[str], *, tree: Optional[ast.AST] = None,
) -> dict[int, list[tuple[int, int]]]:
    """Map 0-based def/class lines to their owned inclusive decorator ranges.

    This is syntax interpretation, NOT beacon attachment policy. Forward
    mapping may skip ordinary leading comments; reverse reconciliation keeps
    its stricter physical-block checks. Both consume these SAME ranges.

    ``tree``, when supplied, must come from the same ``lines`` snapshot.
    Expression linenos can omit the leading @ and wrapping parentheses, so
    complete tokenizer logical statements supply the physical boundaries.
    Parse/tokenization failure returns no extra gap-crossing permission.
    """
    import io
    import tokenize

    source = "\n".join(lines)
    try:
        if tree is None:
            tree = ast.parse(source)
        targets = [
            node for node in ast.walk(tree)
            if isinstance(node, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
            and node.decorator_list
        ]
        if not targets:
            return {}

        # A decorator is a whole logical statement starting with @, not any
        # physical line starting with @ (which could be a continuation).
        candidates: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        last_definition_line = max(node.lineno for node in targets)
        first = None
        for token in tokenize.generate_tokens(io.StringIO(source).readline):
            if token.start[0] >= last_definition_line:
                break
            if token.type in (tokenize.NL, tokenize.COMMENT, tokenize.INDENT, tokenize.DEDENT):
                continue
            if token.type == tokenize.NEWLINE:
                if first is not None and first.type == tokenize.OP and first.string == "@":
                    candidates.append((first.start[0] - 1, token.end[0] - 1))
                first = None
            elif first is None:
                first = token

        by_definition: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int]]] = {}
        for target in targets:
            ranges: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
            for decorator in target.decorator_list:
                owned = [
                    (start, end) for start, end in candidates
                    if start <= decorator.lineno - 1
                    and (decorator.end_lineno or decorator.lineno) - 1 <= end
                    and end < target.lineno - 1
                ]
                if len(owned) != 1 or owned[0] in ranges:
                    break  # Never guess ownership or cross unrelated syntax.
                ranges.append(owned[0])
            else:
                by_definition[target.lineno - 1] = ranges
        return by_definition
    except (SyntaxError, ValueError, tokenize.TokenError):
        return {}


def _python_decorator_line_ranges(
    lines: list[str], target_idx0: int,
) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Single-target adapter over the shared whole-file syntax authority."""
    return _python_decorator_ranges_by_definition(lines).get(target_idx0, [])


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_find_anchor_matched_beacons-anch,
#   role=_find_anchor_matched_beacons(language, lines, beacons, target_idx0),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Shared physical-block anchor matcher for preflight/JIT identity reconciliation and legacy writer idempotency. Python gaps may cross only AST-owned decorator syntax ranges (including stacked/multiline/parenthesized decorators); beacons before, between, or after decorators retain their identity. All other gap rules and cardinality checks stay unchanged: unrelated code is never crossed, and a block embedded inside a decorator expression is not a leading beacon.,
# ]
def _find_anchor_matched_beacons(
    language: str,
    lines: list[str],
    beacons: list[dict],
    target_idx0: int,
) -> list[tuple[dict, int, int]]:
    """Return ``[(beacon, start_idx, end_idx), ...]`` anchored to the target.

    Keep the physical def/class line as the target (also used for creates).
    Only Python gains decorator-aware reachability, from this SAME source
    snapshot. Do not just move the target to the first decorator: existing
    writers also place valid beacon blocks after/between decorators.
    """
    decorator_ranges = (
        _python_decorator_line_ranges(lines, target_idx0)
        if language == "python" and beacons else []
    )
    decorator_lines = {
        idx for start, end in decorator_ranges for idx in range(start, end + 1)
    }
    matches: list[tuple[dict, int, int]] = []
    for b in beacons or []:
        extent = _beacon_block_extent_for_language(language, lines, b)
        if extent is None:
            continue
        start_idx, end_idx = extent
        if end_idx >= target_idx0:
            continue
        if any(start_idx <= end and end_idx >= start for start, end in decorator_ranges):
            continue  # A comment INSIDE a decorator is not a leading beacon.
        anchors = True
        for k in range(end_idx + 1, target_idx0):
            if k in decorator_lines:
                continue
            line_text = lines[k] if k < len(lines) else ""
            if not line_text.strip():
                continue
            if line_text.lstrip().startswith("<!--") or "@beacon" in line_text or line_text.strip() == "-->":
                continue
            anchors = False
            break
        if anchors:
            matches.append((b, start_idx, end_idx))
    return matches


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_resolve_anchor_target_line-resa,
#   role=_resolve_anchor_target_line(language, source_path, lines, ast_qualname, md_path, occurrence),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Resolve a node's anchor target to a 0-based line (AST def line via the PURE outline builders; Markdown heading via _find_markdown_heading_insert_idx with the occurrence ordinal). Returns (idx0, ambiguous): (None, False) = unresolvable (exact-ID identity branch), (None, True) = ambiguous outline match (fail closed). Never writes; never calls a mutating parser.,
# ]
def _resolve_anchor_target_line(
    language: str,
    source_path: str,
    lines: list[str],
    ast_qualname: Optional[str],
    md_path: Optional[list[str]],
    occurrence: Optional[int],
) -> tuple[Optional[int], bool]:
    """Return ``(target_idx0, ambiguous)`` -- the 0-based line of this node's
    anchor target (AST def line / Markdown heading line), or ``None`` when
    unresolvable. ``ambiguous=True`` means MULTIPLE outline matches."""
    if language == "markdown":
        idx = _find_markdown_heading_insert_idx(lines, md_path or [], occurrence)
        return (idx, False) if isinstance(idx, int) and idx >= 0 else (None, False)
    if not ast_qualname:
        return None, False
    try:
        if language == "python":
            outline = parse_file_outline(source_path)
        elif language == "js_ts":
            outline = parse_js_ts_outline(source_path)
        elif language == "vue":
            outline = parse_vue_outline(source_path)
        else:
            return None, False
    except Exception:
        return None, False
    matches = [
        n
        for n in _iter_ast_outline_nodes(outline or [])
        if n.get("ast_qualname") == ast_qualname
    ]
    if len(matches) > 1:
        return None, True
    if len(matches) != 1:
        return None, False
    ln = matches[0].get("orig_lineno_start_unused")
    if isinstance(ln, int) and 0 < ln <= len(lines):
        return ln - 1, False
    return None, False


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lightning Rod beacon mutation -- COMMIT SEAM plan-branch engine (SPEC v5 §6)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _assert_write_intent_plan_pair(write_intent, write_plan) -> None:
    """BOTH-OR-NEITHER contract for the write_intent/write_plan kwargs.

    The legacy F12 paths pass NEITHER (byte-for-byte legacy behavior); the
    Lightning Rod mutation commit seam passes BOTH. An intent without a plan
    is an UNDEFINED combination (reserved for a future mode) -- reject loudly.
    """
    assert (write_intent is None) == (write_plan is None), (
        "write_intent and write_plan are BOTH-OR-NEITHER: an intent without "
        "a plan is an UNDEFINED combination (reserved for a future mode)"
    )


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_build_beacon_block_for_language-bbfl,
#   role=_build_beacon_block_for_language(language, bid, role, slice_labels, kind, show_span, comment_text, start_snippet, end_snippet, section),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,agent-kit-vendor,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Module-level beacon block renderer for the commit-seam plan branch (§6). Covers every language grammar: python/shell/yaml (#), js_ts/vue-script (//), sql (--), markdown/vue-template (<!-- -->), vue-style (/* */). Unlike the legacy per-writer closures it ALSO preserves start_snippet/end_snippet (a labels-only mutation can never drop a span beacon's anchors, §5 step 10) and it SKIPS the note-derived decoration strip / transport-entity decode (explicit plan-resolved values only, §3 fixpoint rules).,
# ]
def _build_beacon_block_for_language(
    language: str,
    *,
    bid: str,
    role: Optional[str],
    slice_labels: Optional[str],
    kind: Optional[str],
    show_span: Optional[bool],
    comment_text: Optional[str],
    start_snippet: Optional[str] = None,
    end_snippet: Optional[str] = None,
    section: Optional[str] = None,
) -> list[str]:
    """Render one beacon block for the language's comment grammar (plan branch).

    ``section`` disambiguates Vue SFC blocks ('script' | 'template' |
    'style'); it is ignored for every other language.
    """
    style = language
    if language == "vue":
        style = {"template": "html", "style": "css", "script": "js_line"}.get(
            section or "script", "js_line"
        )
    elif language == "markdown":
        style = "html"
    elif language == "js_ts":
        style = "js_line"

    fields: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    if role:
        fields.append(("role", str(role)))
    if slice_labels:
        fields.append(("slice_labels", str(slice_labels)))
    if kind:
        fields.append(("kind", str(kind)))
    if start_snippet:
        fields.append(("start_snippet", f'"{start_snippet}"'))
    if end_snippet:
        fields.append(("end_snippet", f'"{end_snippet}"'))
    if show_span is not None:
        fields.append(("show_span", "true" if show_span else "false"))
    if comment_text:
        fields.append(("comment", str(comment_text)))

    if style == "html":
        lines = ["<!--", "@beacon[", f"  id={bid},"]
        lines += [f"  {k}={v}," for k, v in fields]
        lines += ["]", "-->"]
        return lines
    if style == "css":
        lines = ["/* @beacon[", f"   id={bid},"]
        lines += [f"   {k}={v}," for k, v in fields]
        lines += ["] */"]
        return lines
    prefix = {"js_line": "//", "sql": "--"}.get(style) or (
        "--" if language == "sql" else "#"
    )
    lines = [f"{prefix} @beacon[", f"{prefix}   id={bid},"]
    lines += [f"{prefix}   {k}={v}," for k, v in fields]
    lines += [f"{prefix} ]"]
    return lines


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_find_beacon_close_block_extent-fbce,
#   role=_find_beacon_close_block_extent(language, lines, bid, search_from_idx),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,agent-kit-vendor,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=Find the @beacon-close[ ... ] block whose id matches bid and return its 0-based (start, end) extent. §6 ALL-writers DELETE semantics: when deleting a kind=span beacon, a matching close block is deleted too (no orphaned closers).,
# ]
def _find_beacon_close_block_extent(
    language: str,
    lines: list[str],
    bid: str,
    search_from_idx: int = 0,
) -> Optional[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Return the 0-based ``(start_idx, end_idx)`` extent of the
    ``@beacon-close[ ... ]`` block whose id equals ``bid``, or ``None``."""
    prefix = _BEACON_EXTENT_LINE_PREFIX.get(language)
    wanted = (bid or "").strip()
    if not wanted:
        return None
    i = max(0, search_from_idx)
    while i < len(lines):
        if "@beacon-close[" not in lines[i]:
            i += 1
            continue
        found_id = None
        end_idx = i
        j = i
        while j < len(lines):
            body = lines[j].lstrip()
            if prefix and body.startswith(prefix):
                body = body[len(prefix):].lstrip()
            text = body.strip()
            while text and text[-1] in ",]":
                text = text[:-1].rstrip()
            if text.startswith("id="):
                found_id = (
                    text.split("=", 1)[1].strip().strip('"').strip("'").strip()
                )
            if body.strip().startswith("]"):
                end_idx = j
                break
            j += 1
        if found_id == wanted:
            if language == "markdown":
                # HTML-style close blocks: include the surrounding <!-- ... -->
                # wrapper so a span delete leaves NO orphan comment shell
                # behind (SPEC v5 §6: no orphaned closers).
                start_idx = i
                if "<!--" not in lines[i]:
                    k = i
                    while k >= 1:
                        raw = lines[k - 1]
                        if "<!--" in raw:
                            start_idx = k - 1
                            break
                        if raw.strip():
                            break
                        k -= 1
                close_end = end_idx
                if "-->" not in lines[end_idx]:
                    k = end_idx + 1
                    while k < len(lines):
                        if "-->" in lines[k]:
                            close_end = k
                            break
                        k += 1
                return (start_idx, close_end)
            return (i, end_idx)
        i += 1
    return None


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_vue_beacon_section_for_extent-rjjx,
#   role=_vue_beacon_section_for_extent,
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def _vue_beacon_section_for_extent(lines: list[str], start_idx: int) -> str:
    """Classify a Vue beacon block's comment style from its opener line."""
    opener = (lines[start_idx] if 0 <= start_idx < len(lines) else "").lstrip()
    if opener.startswith("<!--"):
        return "template"
    if opener.startswith("/*"):
        return "style"
    return "script"


# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@_execute_beacon_write_plan-exec,
#   role=_execute_beacon_write_plan(language, file_path, name, note, write_intent, write_plan, occurrence),
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
#   comment=THE shared plan-branch execution engine for every language helper (SPEC v5 §6). VERIFIES the plan against live disk state (dispatch path, planned operation, plan-selected physical target, raw-bytes sha256 CAS) -- a sha divergence is source_changed_since_plan, every OTHER divergence is plan_mismatch, both with ZERO write. Then executes the planned operation with the plan-resolved comment, sourcing metadata per §5 step 10: slice_labels + comment from the plan; role/kind/show_span/start/end snippets from the FRESH DISK PARSE (a labels-only mutation never rewrites them). 2R4E1P: the plan's comment decision is authoritative -- fill keeps an existing comment (a kept conflict's resolved comment IS the on-disk text; only a fill plan that would overwrite a DIFFERENT live comment is refused, existing_comment_present/plan_mismatch); replace/append overwrite/extend explicitly. Span deletes also remove the matching @beacon-close[ block. Exactly ONE atomic replacement via _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(expected_sha256=...).,
# ]
def _execute_beacon_write_plan(
    *,
    language: str,
    file_path: str,
    name: str,
    note: str,
    write_intent,
    write_plan,
    occurrence: Optional[int] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Verify + execute a BeaconWritePlan against live disk state (§6).

    Mismatch semantics (contract): a raw-bytes SHA divergence is
    ``source_changed_since_plan``; EVERY OTHER divergence (source path,
    planned operation, selected physical target) is ``plan_mismatch``.
    Either way ``{success: False, ...}`` with ZERO bytes written.
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "language": language,
        "file_path": file_path,
        "operation": "noop",
        "planned_operation": getattr(write_plan, "planned_operation", None),
    }
    planned_op = getattr(write_plan, "planned_operation", None)
    if planned_op in (None, "noop"):
        return result
    if not getattr(write_plan, "success", False):
        result["error"] = getattr(write_plan, "error", None) or "invalid write plan"
        result["error_kind"] = getattr(write_plan, "error_kind", None) or "plan_mismatch"
        return result

    planned_sha = getattr(write_plan, "source_sha256", None)
    planned_path = getattr(write_plan, "source_path", None)
    if planned_path and os.path.normcase(os.path.realpath(file_path)) != os.path.normcase(
        os.path.realpath(str(planned_path))
    ):
        result["error"] = (
            f"plan_mismatch: planned source path {planned_path!r} != "
            f"dispatch path {file_path!r}"
        )
        result["error_kind"] = "plan_mismatch"
        return result

    # CAS pre-check (the atomic writer re-checks at the final moment).
    if planned_sha:
        try:
            with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
                current_bytes = f.read()
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            result["error"] = f"failed_to_read_file: {e}"
            result["error_kind"] = "disk_read_failed"
            return result
        if hashlib.sha256(current_bytes).hexdigest() != planned_sha:
            result["error"] = (
                "source_changed_since_plan: raw-bytes sha256 mismatch; "
                "zero bytes written"
            )
            result["error_kind"] = "source_changed_since_plan"
            return result

    try:
        lines, beacons = _parse_disk_beacons_for_language(language, file_path)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        result["error"] = f"failed_to_read_file: {e}"
        result["error_kind"] = "disk_read_failed"
        return result

    intended_labels = list(getattr(write_plan, "intended_slice_labels", ()) or ())
    resolved_comment = getattr(write_plan, "resolved_comment", None)
    bid = (getattr(write_plan, "beacon_id", None) or "").strip() or None
    operation = "noop"
    section = "script"

    if planned_op in ("update", "delete"):
        matches = [b for b in beacons if (b.get("id") or "").strip() == (bid or "")]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            result["error"] = (
                f"plan_mismatch: planned target beacon id {bid!r} matches "
                f"{len(matches)} blocks on disk"
            )
            result["error_kind"] = "plan_mismatch"
            return result
        sel = matches[0]
        extent = _beacon_block_extent_for_language(language, lines, sel)
        if extent is None:
            result["error"] = f"plan_mismatch: cannot compute extent of beacon id {bid!r}"
            result["error_kind"] = "plan_mismatch"
            return result
        start_idx, end_idx = extent
        if language == "vue":
            section = _vue_beacon_section_for_extent(lines, start_idx)
            if section != "script":
                # Defense-in-depth behind the plan-seam capability gate: the
                # shared extent layer covers only the vue-script (//) comment
                # grammar; never write a template/style block from a plan.
                result["error"] = (
                    "operation_not_supported_for_target: Vue template/style "
                    "beacon mutation is not supported in v1 (vue-script only)"
                )
                result["error_kind"] = "operation_not_supported_for_target"
                return result

        if planned_op == "delete":
            ranges = [(start_idx, end_idx)]
            if (sel.get("kind") or "").strip().lower() == "span":
                close_extent = _find_beacon_close_block_extent(
                    language, lines, bid, search_from_idx=end_idx + 1
                )
                if close_extent:
                    ranges.append(close_extent)
            new_lines = [
                line
                for i, line in enumerate(lines)
                if not any(a <= i <= b for a, b in ranges)
            ]
            operation = "deleted_beacon"
        else:
            # Comment defense-in-depth (§6), 2R4E1P: the plan's comment
            # decision is AUTHORITATIVE -- a fill_if_empty conflict is KEPT
            # (resolved_comment IS the existing comment, labels still
            # ensured) and replace/append overwrite/extend explicitly.
            # Refuse only when a fill plan would overwrite a DIFFERENT live
            # on-disk comment than the one it kept -- a state the sha256
            # CAS should already have caught.
            if (
                write_intent is not None
                and getattr(write_intent.comment, "mode", None) == "fill_if_empty"
            ):
                live_comment = sel.get("comment")
                if (live_comment or "").strip() and (resolved_comment or "") != live_comment:
                    result["error"] = "existing_comment_present"
                    result["existing_comment"] = live_comment
                    result["error_kind"] = "plan_mismatch"
                    return result
            # §5 step 10: slice_labels + comment from the plan; role/kind/
            # show_span/start/end snippets from the FRESH DISK PARSE.
            new_block = _build_beacon_block_for_language(
                language,
                bid=bid,
                role=sel.get("role"),
                slice_labels=",".join(intended_labels),
                kind=(sel.get("kind") or "ast"),
                show_span=(bool(sel.get("show_span")) if sel.get("show_span_explicit") else None),
                comment_text=resolved_comment,
                start_snippet=sel.get("start_snippet"),
                end_snippet=sel.get("end_snippet"),
                section=section,
            )
            if language != "markdown":
                new_block = _indent_beacon_block(new_block, lines, start_idx)
            new_lines = lines[:start_idx] + new_block + lines[end_idx + 1:]
            operation = "updated_beacon"
    else:  # create
        md_path = None
        ast_qualname = None
        if language == "markdown":
            md_path = _extract_md_path_from_note(note)
            occ = getattr(write_plan, "occurrence", None)
            if occ is None:
                occ = occurrence
            target_idx0 = _find_markdown_heading_insert_idx(lines, md_path or [], occ)
        else:
            ast_qualname = _extract_ast_qualname_from_note(note)
            target_idx0 = None
            if ast_qualname:
                target_idx0, _amb = _resolve_anchor_target_line(
                    language, file_path, lines, ast_qualname, None, None
                )
        if not isinstance(target_idx0, int) or target_idx0 < 0:
            result["error"] = "plan_mismatch: the planned create anchor no longer resolves"
            result["error_kind"] = "plan_mismatch"
            return result
        new_id = bid
        if not new_id:
            if language == "markdown":
                new_id = f"auto-beacon@{_generate_auto_beacon_hash()}{_generate_auto_beacon_hash()}"
            else:
                new_id = f"auto-beacon@{ast_qualname}-{_generate_auto_beacon_hash()}"
        # create-path seeding: role comes from the cached name/note (§5 step 10).
        if language == "markdown":
            base_name, _t = split_name_and_tags(name)
            role_seed = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(base_name) or (
                md_path[-1] if md_path else "md"
            )
        else:
            role_seed = ast_qualname
        new_block = _build_beacon_block_for_language(
            language,
            bid=new_id,
            role=role_seed,
            slice_labels=",".join(intended_labels),
            kind="ast",
            show_span=None,
            comment_text=resolved_comment,
            section=section,
        )
        new_block = _indent_beacon_block(new_block, lines, target_idx0)
        new_lines = lines[:target_idx0] + new_block + lines[target_idx0:]
        operation = "created_beacon"
        bid = new_id

    try:
        _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines, expected_sha256=planned_sha or None)
    except SourceChangedSincePlanError as e:
        result["error"] = str(e)
        result["error_kind"] = "source_changed_since_plan"
        return result
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
        return result

    result.update(
        {
            "success": True,
            "operation": operation,
            "beacon_id": bid,
            "slice_labels": ",".join(intended_labels),
            "comment": resolved_comment,
        }
    )
    return result


# @beacon[
#   id=carto-js-ts@update_beacon_from_node_python,
#   role=🔱 🔱 carto-js-ts,
#   slice_labels=carto-js-ts,carto-js-ts-beacons,ra-reconcile,nexus--tags,
#   kind=span,
# ]
# Python: update/create/delete a beacon for a single AST/beacon node
# based on Workflowy name/note fields.
#
# This is the Cartographer-side helper invoked by the MCP server when F12
# is pressed on a Python AST/beacon node. It does not talk to Workflowy
# directly; instead it focuses on:
#   • interpreting Workflowy name/note as desired beacon metadata
#   • computing canonical slice_labels from tags + note
#   • deciding whether to update, create, or delete @beacon[...] blocks
#   • returning a small summary of what should be reflected back into
#     the local cache.
# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@update_beacon_from_node_python-1svv,
#   role=update_beacon_from_node_python,
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def update_beacon_from_node_python(
    file_path: str,
    name: str,
    note: str,
    *,
    write_intent=None,
    write_plan=None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Update/create/delete Python @beacon[...] blocks for a single node.

    This helper performs on-disk edits ONLY. It does not touch Workflowy; the
    MCP client is responsible for updating the local /nodes-export cache name
    (and possibly note) to reflect the final state returned here.

    Policy:
    - If note contains BEACON metadata with an id: → update existing block.
    - Else if AST_QUALNAME present and tags present → create a new beacon
      anchored to that AST node, derived from tags.
    - Else if there is a beacon on disk for this AST_QUALNAME/id but the note
      has no BEACON block → delete the beacon.

    Source-of-truth policy (May 2026 sweep, applies to all language helpers):
      role         <- Workflowy node name (decoration-stripped); metadata `role:` IGNORED.
      slice_labels <- Workflowy node name #tags (only); metadata `slice_labels:` IGNORED.
      comment      <- metadata block `comment:` / `comments:` line (still source of truth).
      kind / show_span <- metadata block (low-traffic invariants).
    The Workflowy NAME field is authoritative for role and tags; the metadata
    block is treated as a stale snapshot for those fields. Reading role from
    the metadata caused a data-loss bug where renames in Workflowy were
    silently reverted by the subsequent disk -> Workflowy reconcile.

    PLAN BRANCH (SPEC v5 §6): when ``write_intent`` + ``write_plan`` are
    supplied (BOTH-or-neither, asserted), execution delegates to
    ``_execute_beacon_write_plan`` -- the plan's independent reconciliation
    is authoritative and the legacy note-first rediscovery never runs.
    """
    _assert_write_intent_plan_pair(write_intent, write_plan)
    if write_plan is not None:
        return _execute_beacon_write_plan(
            language="python",
            file_path=file_path,
            name=name,
            note=note,
            write_intent=write_intent,
            write_plan=write_plan,
        )

    base_name, tags = split_name_and_tags(name)
    beacon_id = _extract_beacon_id_from_note(note)
    ast_qualname = _extract_ast_qualname_from_note(note)

    # Role-from-name: strip Workflowy trident decoration from base_name so the
    # on-disk beacon's role: line reflects the user's typed heading text.
    role_from_name = base_name
    if role_from_name:
        while role_from_name.endswith("\U0001F531"):
            role_from_name = role_from_name[:-1].rstrip()
        role_from_name = role_from_name.strip()
        # Also strip any LEADING decoration symbols (trident / AST-type
        # emoji) an agent may have pasted into the node name, so the
        # on-disk role: never carries decoration that would compound on
        # the next deterministic F12 refresh.
        role_from_name = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(role_from_name)

    print(
        f"[CARTOGRAPHER] update_beacon_from_node_python start "
        f"(file_path={file_path!r}, base_name={base_name!r}, "
        f"tags={tags}, beacon_id={beacon_id!r}, ast_qualname={ast_qualname!r})",
        file=sys.stderr,
    )

    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "language": "python",
        "file_path": file_path,
        "base_name": base_name,
        "tags": tags,
        "beacon_id": beacon_id,
        "ast_qualname": ast_qualname,
        "operation": "noop",
    }

    # Load file lines once.
    try:
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lines = f.read().splitlines()
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        result["error"] = f"failed_to_read_file: {e}"
        return result

    beacons = parse_python_beacon_blocks(lines)

    # Helper: locate beacon block by id and return (start_idx,end_idx,comment_line).
    def _find_beacon_block_by_id(bid: str) -> Optional[tuple[int, int, int]]:
        bid = (bid or "").strip()
        if not bid:
            return None
        for b in beacons:
            if (b.get("id") or "").strip() != bid:
                continue
            cl = b.get("comment_line")
            if not isinstance(cl, int) or cl <= 0:
                continue
            # Walk forward to find the closing ']' line; include all lines that
            # start with '#' and belong to this metadata block.
            #
            # We treat a line as the closing delimiter only when the metadata
            # body is exactly a single ']' after stripping the leading '#' and
            # whitespace. This prevents an incidental ']' inside a comment
            # field from terminating the block prematurely.
            start_idx = cl - 1
            j = cl
            end_idx = start_idx
            while j <= len(lines):
                raw = lines[j - 1]
                stripped = raw.lstrip()
                if stripped.startswith("#"):
                    body = stripped.lstrip("#").lstrip()
                else:
                    body = stripped
                if body == "]":
                    end_idx = j - 1
                    break
                j += 1
            return start_idx, end_idx, cl
        return None

    # Helper: rebuild a beacon block for a given id/role/slice_labels/comment.
    def _build_beacon_block(
        bid: str,
        role: str,
        slice_labels: str,
        kind: str,
        show_span: Optional[bool],
        comment_text: Optional[str],
    ) -> list[str]:
        meta_lines = [
            "# @beacon[",
            f"#   id={bid},",
        ]
        if role:
            meta_lines.append(f"#   role={role},")
        if slice_labels:
            meta_lines.append(f"#   slice_labels={slice_labels},")
        if kind:
            meta_lines.append(f"#   kind={kind},")
        if show_span is not None:
            meta_lines.append(f"#   show_span={'true' if show_span else 'false'},")
        if comment_text:
            meta_lines.append(f"#   comment={comment_text},")
        meta_lines.append("# ]")
        return meta_lines

    # Case 1: beacon_id present in note (update existing or create from metadata).
    if beacon_id:
        # See the helper docstring for the source-of-truth policy. Comment is
        # the only field still parsed from the note metadata block.
        comment_val: str | None = None
        for line in (note or "").splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if stripped.startswith("comment:") or stripped.startswith("comments:"):
                comment_val = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(stripped.split(":", 1)[1].strip())

        show_span_note = _extract_show_span_from_note(note)

        # Role comes from the live Workflowy node name (decoration-stripped),
        # NOT from the cached note's stale BEACON metadata.
        role_display = role_from_name or (beacon_id.split("@", 1)[0] if "@" in beacon_id else "")

        # Slice labels come **only** from Workflowy tags in the node name.
        extra_label_tokens = [t.lstrip("#") for t in tags]
        slice_labels_canon = (
            _canonicalize_slice_labels(",".join(extra_label_tokens), None)
            if extra_label_tokens
            else ""
        )

        block_span = _find_beacon_block_by_id(beacon_id)
        if block_span is not None:
            start_idx, end_idx, _cl = block_span

            # Tag-vanish DELETE path: if Workflowy name has NO trailing #tags,
            # delete the on-disk beacon block (regardless of kind=ast or kind=span).
            # Span beacons with no slice_labels are useless (cannot appear in
            # any context slice filter), so removing them when tags vanish is
            # the correct UX -- it lets users delete beacons by simply removing
            # the tag from the Workflowy node name.
            if not tags:
                # §6 ALL writers: a kind=span delete also removes the
                # matching @beacon-close[ block (no orphaned closers).
                ranges = [(start_idx, end_idx)]
                _del_kind = ""
                for _b in beacons:
                    if (_b.get("id") or "").strip() == (beacon_id or "").strip():
                        _del_kind = (_b.get("kind") or "").strip().lower()
                        break
                if _del_kind == "span":
                    _close = _find_beacon_close_block_extent(
                        result["language"], lines, beacon_id, search_from_idx=end_idx + 1
                    )
                    if _close:
                        ranges.append(_close)
                new_lines = [
                    line
                    for i, line in enumerate(lines)
                    if not any(a <= i <= b for a, b in ranges)
                ]
                try:
                    _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines)
                except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
                    result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
                    return result

                result.update(
                    {
                        "operation": "deleted_beacon",
                        "beacon_id": beacon_id,
                        "role": role_display or "",
                        "slice_labels": "",
                    }
                )
                return result

            # Case 1a: UPDATE existing beacon on disk.

            # Preserve existing kind (span vs ast) when updating an on-disk beacon.
            existing_kind = "ast"
            existing_show_span: Optional[bool] = None
            for b in beacons:
                if (b.get("id") or "").strip() != (beacon_id or "").strip():
                    continue
                k_val = (b.get("kind") or "").strip().lower()
                if k_val in {"ast", "span"}:
                    existing_kind = k_val
                # Preserve explicit show_span from disk when present.
                if b.get("show_span_explicit"):
                    existing_show_span = bool(b.get("show_span"))
                break

            show_span_to_write: Optional[bool] = (
                show_span_note if show_span_note is not None else existing_show_span
            )

            new_block = _build_beacon_block(
                bid=beacon_id,
                role=role_display or "",
                slice_labels=slice_labels_canon,
                kind=existing_kind,
                show_span=show_span_to_write,
                comment_text=comment_val,
            )
            new_block = _indent_beacon_block(new_block, lines, start_idx)
            new_lines = lines[:start_idx] + new_block + lines[end_idx + 1 :]
            try:
                _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines)
            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
                result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
                return result

            result.update(
                {
                    "operation": "updated_beacon",
                    "role": role_display,
                    "slice_labels": slice_labels_canon,
                    "comment": comment_val,
                }
            )
            return result
        else:
            # Beacon id present in Workflowy note but no matching block on disk.
            # (Formerly Case 1b: CREATE beacon from note metadata.) This path was
            # used when a user pasted a BEACON metadata block into a Workflowy
            # note to materialize an AST beacon on disk. Removed as deprecated:
            # AST beacons should be created via the simpler tags-only path
            # (Case 2). Treat as noop.
            return result

    # Helper: delete beacon(s) for this AST_QUALNAME using Cartographer outline.
    #
    # We intentionally fail closed: if we cannot unambiguously map the
    # AST_QUALNAME to exactly one AST node (with a BEACON block in its note),
    # we do not delete anything.
    def _delete_beacon_for_ast_qualname(target_ast_qualname: str) -> Optional[str]:
        try:
            outline_nodes = parse_file_outline(file_path)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            print(
                "[CARTOGRAPHER] update_beacon_from_node_python delete-abort "
                f"(file_path={file_path!r}, ast_qualname={target_ast_qualname!r}, error={e!r})",
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            return None

        if not outline_nodes:
            return None

        # Find the unique AST node for this qualname.
        matches: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            n
            for n in _iter_ast_outline_nodes(outline_nodes)
            if n.get("ast_qualname") == target_ast_qualname
        ]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            # Ambiguous or missing mapping – do nothing.
            print(
                "[CARTOGRAPHER] update_beacon_from_node_python delete-abort "
                f"(file_path={file_path!r}, ast_qualname={target_ast_qualname!r}, "
                f"matches={len(matches)})",
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            return None

        node = matches[0]
        node_note = node.get("note") or ""
        if "BEACON (" not in node_note:
            return None

        bid = _extract_beacon_id_from_note(node_note)
        if not bid:
            return None

        span = _find_beacon_block_by_id(bid)
        if span is None:
            return None

        start_idx, end_idx, _cl = span
        ranges = [(start_idx, end_idx)]
        _del_kind = ""
        for _b in beacons:
            if (_b.get("id") or "").strip() == (bid or "").strip():
                _del_kind = (_b.get("kind") or "").strip().lower()
                break
        if _del_kind == "span":
            _close = _find_beacon_close_block_extent(
                result["language"], lines, bid, search_from_idx=end_idx + 1
            )
            if _close:
                ranges.append(_close)
        new_lines = [
            line
            for i, line in enumerate(lines)
            if not any(a <= i <= b for a, b in ranges)
        ]
        try:
            _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
            return None

        return bid

    # Case 1c: DELETE beacon when note has no BEACON metadata and no tags.
    # Fallback safety net: if the user manually deleted the BEACON block from
    # the Workflowy note (so beacon_id is empty) AND removed the tags from the
    # name, we still walk the on-disk source and remove the orphaned beacon
    # for this AST_QUALNAME. The primary tag-vanish DELETE path now lives in
    # Case 1a above (when beacon_id is still in the note).
    if ast_qualname and not tags and not beacon_id:
        deleted_id = _delete_beacon_for_ast_qualname(ast_qualname)
        if deleted_id:
            result.update(
                {
                    "operation": "deleted_beacon",
                    "beacon_id": deleted_id,
                    "role": ast_qualname,
                    "slice_labels": "",
                }
            )
            return result

    # Case 2: no beacon_id, but AST_QUALNAME + tags → create a new beacon.
    if ast_qualname and tags:
        # Build a synthetic id with a hash suffix for collision resistance.
        hash_suffix = _generate_auto_beacon_hash()
        simple_id = f"auto-beacon@{ast_qualname}-{hash_suffix}"
        role_display = ast_qualname

        # Slice labels come **only** from Workflowy tags; we do not auto-derive
        # additional labels from role/id/AST_QUALNAME.
        extra_label_tokens = [t.lstrip("#") for t in tags]
        slice_labels_canon = (
            _canonicalize_slice_labels(",".join(extra_label_tokens), None)
            if extra_label_tokens
            else ""
        )

        # Insert above the AST node header line (best-effort): use
        # parse_file_outline to discover the node and its start line.
        try:
            outline_nodes = parse_file_outline(file_path)
        except Exception:
            outline_nodes = []

        target_line: Optional[int] = None
        if outline_nodes:
            # Walk outline to find node with matching AST_QUALNAME.
            def _iter_nodes(nodes_list: list[dict[str, Any]]):
                for n in nodes_list:
                    if isinstance(n, dict):
                        yield n
                        for ch in n.get("children") or []:
                            if isinstance(ch, dict):
                                yield from _iter_nodes([ch])

            for n in _iter_nodes(outline_nodes):
                if n.get("ast_qualname") == ast_qualname:
                    ln = n.get("orig_lineno_start_unused")
                    if isinstance(ln, int) and ln > 0:
                        target_line = ln
                        break

        if not isinstance(target_line, int) or target_line <= 0 or target_line > len(lines):
            # FAIL-CLOSED (SPEC v5 §6, commit 1): an unresolved AST anchor is
            # an error with ZERO bytes written -- NEVER a top-of-file
            # fallback insertion (invariant 6: fail-closed anchoring).
            print(
                "[CARTOGRAPHER] update_beacon_from_node_python create-abort "
                f"(file_path={file_path!r}, ast_qualname={ast_qualname!r}): "
                "AST_QUALNAME did not resolve to an outline node; refusing "
                "to fall back to top-of-file insertion.",
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            result["error"] = (
                f"ast_qualname_unresolved: AST_QUALNAME {ast_qualname!r} did "
                f"not resolve to an outline node in {file_path!r}; refusing "
                f"to fall back to top-of-file insertion."
            )
            return result
        insert_idx = target_line - 1

        # IDEMPOTENCY GUARD (SPEC v5 §6, ported from the Markdown writer):
        # if a beacon block already anchors this AST target, UPDATE it in
        # place with the existing id instead of creating a duplicate block.
        _anchored = _find_anchor_matched_beacons("python", lines, beacons, insert_idx)
        if _anchored:
            _ab, _a_start, _a_end = _anchored[0]
            _existing_id = (_ab.get("id") or "").strip()
            if _existing_id:
                _existing_kind = (_ab.get("kind") or "ast").strip().lower()
                if _existing_kind not in {"ast", "span"}:
                    _existing_kind = "ast"
                _existing_show_span = (
                    bool(_ab.get("show_span")) if _ab.get("show_span_explicit") else None
                )
                new_block = _build_beacon_block(
                    bid=_existing_id,
                    role=role_display,
                    slice_labels=slice_labels_canon,
                    kind=_existing_kind,
                    show_span=_existing_show_span,
                    comment_text=None,
                )
                new_block = _indent_beacon_block(new_block, lines, _a_start)
                new_lines = lines[:_a_start] + new_block + lines[_a_end + 1:]
                try:
                    _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines)
                except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
                    result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
                    return result
                result.update(
                    {
                        "operation": "updated_beacon",
                        "beacon_id": _existing_id,
                        "role": role_display,
                        "slice_labels": slice_labels_canon,
                    }
                )
                return result

        # The synthetic comment marker was deleted (SPEC v5 commit 4):
        # legacy Case-2 creation writes NO comment; mutation-path comments
        # arrive RESOLVED via write_intent on the plan branch.
        _comment_val: str | None = None

        new_block = _build_beacon_block(
            bid=simple_id,
            role=role_display,
            slice_labels=slice_labels_canon,
            kind="ast",
            show_span=None,
            comment_text=_comment_val,
        )
        new_block = _indent_beacon_block(new_block, lines, insert_idx)
        new_lines = lines[:insert_idx] + new_block + lines[insert_idx:]
        try:
            _write_markdown_lines_sanitized(file_path, new_lines)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            result["error"] = f"failed_to_write_file: {e}"
            return result

        result.update(
            {
                "operation": "created_beacon",
                "beacon_id": simple_id,
                "role": role_display,
                "slice_labels": slice_labels_canon,
                "comment": _comment_val,
            }
        )
        return result

    # Otherwise, noop for now.
    print(
        "[CARTOGRAPHER] update_beacon_from_node_python noop "
        f"(file_path={file_path!r}, base_name={base_name!r}, "
        f"tags={tags}, beacon_id={beacon_id!r}, ast_qualname={ast_qualname!r})",
        file=sys.stderr,
    )
    return result


# @beacon[
#   id=carto-js-ts@update_beacon_from_node_js_ts,
#   role=🔱 🔱 carto-js-ts,
#   slice_labels=carto-js-ts,carto-js-ts-beacons,ra-reconcile,nexus--tags,
#   kind=span,
# ]
# JS/TS: update/create/delete a beacon for a single AST/beacon node based
# on Workflowy name/note fields. Mirrors the Python helper but uses
# JS/TS-specific outline + beacon parsing.
# @beacon[
#   id=auto-beacon@update_beacon_from_node_js_ts-d5kl,
#   role=update_beacon_from_node_js_ts,
#   slice_labels=beacon-writeback,
#   kind=ast,
# ]
def update_beacon_from_node_js_ts(
    file_path: str,
    name: str,
    note: str,
    *,
    write_intent=None,
    write_plan=None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Update/create/delete JS/TS @beacon[...] blocks for a single node.

    Mirrors the Python helper but uses JS/TS beacons and Tree-sitter outlines.
    See update_beacon_from_node_python for the source-of-truth policy.
    """
    _assert_write_intent_plan_pair(write_intent, write_plan)
    if write_plan is not None:
        return _execute_beacon_write_plan(
            language="js_ts",
            file_path=file_path,
            name=name,
            note=note,
            write_intent=write_intent,
            write_plan=write_plan,
        )

    base_name, tags = split_name_and_tags(name)
    beacon_id = _extract_beacon_id_from_note(note)
    ast_qualname = _extract_ast_qualname_from_note(note)

    # Role-from-name: strip Workflowy trident decoration from base_name.
    # (Was missing in earlier April 2026 partial fix; referenced as
    # role_from_name below without ever being defined -- latent NameError.
    # Sweep restored it for every language helper, May 2026.)
    role_from_name = base_name
    if role_from_name:
        while role_from_name.endswith("\U0001F531"):
            role_from_name = role_from_name[:-1].rstrip()
        role_from_name = role_from_name.strip()
        # Also strip any LEADING decoration symbols (trident / AST-type
        # emoji) an agent may have pasted into the node name, so the
        # on-disk role: never carries decoration that would compound on
        # the next deterministic F12 refresh.
        role_from_name = strip_leading_decoration_symbols(role_from_name)

    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "language": "js_ts",
        "file_path": file_path,
        "base_name": base_name,
        "tags": tags,
        "beacon_id": beacon_id,
        "ast_qualname": ast_qualname,
        "operation": "noop",
    }

    try:
        with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            lines = f.read().splitlines()
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        result["error"] = f"failed_to_read_file: {e}"
        return result

    beacons = parse_js_beacon_blocks(lines)

    def _find_beacon_block_by_id(bid: str) -> Optional[tuple[int, int, int]]:
        bid = (bid or "").strip()
        if not bid:
            return None
        for b in beacons:
            if (b.get("id") or "").strip() != bid:
                continue
            cl = b.get("comment_line")
            if not isinstance(cl, int) or cl <= 0:
                continue
            # Walk forward to find the closing ']' line; allow for leading
            # '//' comment markers. As with Python, we require the metadata
            # body (after stripping comment sugar and whitespace) to be
            # exactly ']'.
            start_idx = cl - 1
            j = cl
            end_idx = start_idx
            while j <= len(lines):
                raw = lines[j - 1]
                stripped = raw.lstrip()
                if stripped.startswith("//"):
                    body = stripped[2:].lstrip()
                else:
                    body = stripped
                if body == "]":
                    end_idx = j - 1
                    break
                j += 1
            return start_idx, end_idx, cl
        return None

    def _build_beacon_block(
        bid: str,
        role: str,
        slice_labels: str,
        kind: str,
        show_span: Optional[bool],
        comment_text: Optional[str],
    ) -> list[str]:
        meta_lines = [
            "// @beacon[",
            f"//   id={bid},",
        ]
        if role:
            meta_lines.append(f"//   role={role},")
        if slice_labels:
            meta_lines.append(f"//   slice_labels={slice_labels},")
        if kind:
            meta_lines.append(f"//   kind={kind},")
        if show_span is not None:
